Title: Материалы для истории раскола за первое время его существования, под редакцией Н. Субботина; том четвертый: сочинения первых расколоучителей, часть первая (vol. IV: Works of the First Schism-Teachers, part 1)
Creator: Edited by Nikolai Ivanovich Subbotin (1827-1905); the petitions of Nikita Dobrynin (Pustosviat), the priest Lazar and others
Publisher: Moscow: Bratstvo sv. Petra mitropolita
Publication Date: 1878
Rights: Public Domain (published 1878, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: rus

Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Scan contributed to the Internet Archive without a named library; Cyrillic OCR good on the texts.
Content note: The petitions of the first schism-teachers: Nikita Dobrynin's great petition, Lazar's petition and related writings, with Subbotin's introductions; the founding arguments for the old rite, in their authors' own words.

нтся кресть Христовг. Святый Григорй Богослов

Парю благородну, почто нови учители истощили 
крестъь Христовъ, о нихже Павель Апостолъ плача 
глаголетъ, враги ихъ наричетъь креста Христова,
